"""New reference-only free-flight COM invariant; no physical/raw/model read."""
from pathlib import Path
import os,sys,json,hashlib,datetime,traceback
os.environ['CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES']=''
D=Path(__file__).resolve().parent;T=D.parent
def put(p,x):
    with p.open('x',encoding='utf-8') as f:json.dump(x,f,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2,allow_nan=False)
def main():
    put(D/'CONSUMED_BEFORE_NEW_FREE_AIR_COM_PURPOSE.json',dict(purpose='mod4.reference147.own_mass_weighted_COM_freeair_feasibility',utc=datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat(),no_PT_raw_NPZ_SDK_GPU_or_remote=True))
    reads=[]
    def read(p,sha):
        b=p.read_bytes();s=hashlib.sha256(b).hexdigest();reads.append(dict(path=str(p),bytes=len(b),SHA=s,new_numerical_reads=1));assert s==sha;return json.loads(b)
    try:
        ref=read(T/'H1_MATLAB144_REFERENCE_ACTUAL_V4_LF_REPAIR_20261030/OWN_MATLAB144_PACKED73_REFERENCE_KINEMATIC_NOT_VALIDATED.json','3d4d9acac6b5f7b2bd71ffea7fe4ce9a7156962f72f7819d6ef1aec29cc59ae3')
        B=T/'MOD4_H1_FUDAN133_LINE_Z_RESOURCE_V134_20261030/payloads_v2/seed27'
        C=read(B/'job0/assets/import_contract.json','517cd6a4cc469455af08da75dc4b7f7cb596b375bafb4c3e566c7df5bc51df49')
        G=read(B/'source/OWN_STEP_WHEEL_GEOMETRY_FROM_R2_SOURCE.json','461f6b44e6014c72a755a0db5136fa8d45bd5b53b6318ee4b38221a4c597bc4d')
        sys.path.insert(0,str(B/'source'));import torch
        from own_step_differentiable_geometry_v1 import OwnStepWheelGeometry
        from jump_process_geometry122_v1 import OwnProcessGeometry
        from supermimic_wheel_leg_v3.geometry import unflatten_state
        assert not torch.cuda.is_initialized();rng=torch.random.get_rng_state().clone()
        table=torch.tensor(ref['packed73'],dtype=torch.float32);s=unflatten_state(torch.cat((table,torch.zeros(2001,54)),-1))
        wheel=OwnStepWheelGeometry(C,G,ref['joint_names']);proc=OwnProcessGeometry(G,list(wheel.body_names))
        with torch.no_grad():
            g=wheel(s);mass=torch.tensor([C['bodies'][n]['mass_kg'] for n in wheel.body_names]);local=torch.tensor([C['bodies'][n]['com_m'] for n in wheel.body_names]);total=float(mass.sum())
            bodyCOM=g['body_position']+(g['body_rotation']@local[...,None]).squeeze(-1)
            com=(bodyCOM*mass[None,:,None]).sum(1)/mass.sum()
            accel=(com[2:]-2*com[1:-1]+com[:-2])/.01**2
            required=total*(accel-torch.tensor([0.,0.,-9.81]))
            phase=ref['metadata']['phase_id'];lo=g['low'][...,2].amin(-1);clear=proc.nonwheel_clearance(g)
            # Avoid 30ms around phase boundaries; this is a declared smooth-air
            # necessary-condition test, not an unsampled contact/impact claim.
            ids=[k for k in range(3,1998) if all(int(phase[k+j])==7 for j in range(-3,4)) and float(lo[k])>.005 and float(clear[k])>.001]
            rows=[dict(age=k,time_s=.01*k,COM_world=com[k].tolist(),COM_acceleration_xyz=accel[k-1].tolist(),required_external_force_beyond_gravity_N=required[k-1].tolist(),wheel_bottom_min_m=float(lo[k]),nonwheel_proxy_clearance_m=float(clear[k])) for k in ids]
            norms=[sum(v*v for v in r['required_external_force_beyond_gravity_N'])**.5 for r in rows]
            worst=rows[max(range(len(norms)),key=norms.__getitem__)] if rows else None
            values=sorted(norms)
        assert torch.equal(rng,torch.random.get_rng_state()) and not torch.cuda.is_initialized()
        result=dict(passed_diagnosis=True,reference_physical_teacher_valid=False,reads=reads,total_own_mass_kg=total,finite_difference_dt_s=.01,reference_generation_used_wheel_height_ballistic_not_COM=True,air_rows=len(rows),required_force_norm_min_N=min(norms) if norms else None,required_force_norm_median_N=values[len(values)//2] if values else None,required_force_norm_max_N=max(norms) if norms else None,worst=worst,rows=rows,
            scope='Own31body mass-weighted reference COM finite-difference necessary free-flight condition; nominal authored geometry/phase, no measured contact/torque/PhysX validation',

        child={r['child']:(name,r) for name,r in C['joints'].items()}
        def rotation(q):
            w,x,y,z=np.asarray(q)/np.linalg.norm(q)
            return np.array([[1-2*(y*y+z*z),2*(x*y-w*z),2*(x*z+w*y)],
                [2*(x*y+w*z),1-2*(x*x+z*z),2*(y*z-w*x)],
                [2*(x*z-w*y),2*(y*z+w*x),1-2*(x*x+y*y)]])
        def fk(row):
            poses={'chassis':(row[:3],rotation(row[3:7]))};values=dict(zip(qnames,row[13:43]))
            for name in body_names[1:]:
                joint,r=child[name];pp,pr=poses[r['parent']];a=values[joint]
                A=pr@rotation(r['local_rot0'])
                motion=np.array([[1,0,0],[0,np.cos(a),-np.sin(a)],[0,np.sin(a),np.cos(a)]]) if r['type']=='hinge' else np.eye(3)
                rr=A@motion@rotation(r['local_rot1']).T
                p=pp+pr@np.asarray(r['local_pos0'])-rr@np.asarray(r['local_pos1'])
                if r['type']=='slide':p=p+A[:,0]*a
                poses[name]=(p,rr)
            return poses
        ages=[493,540,571,584,594,605,622,642,677]
        poses=[fk(table[k]) for k in ages]
        # These are the same 277 baked actual MOD4 visual meshes already used
        # in the user's true-MOD4 display; no robot substitution or rescaling.
        base=T/'H1_USER_MUJOCO_REAL_BLUE_IMAGINATION_V115_20261029/actual_training_USD_visuals'
        source_xml=(base/'live_display.xml').read_bytes();root0=ET.fromstring(source_xml)
        asset0=root0.find('asset');bodies={x.get('name'):x for x in root0.find('worldbody').findall('body')}
        assert set(bodies)==set(body_names)
        root=ET.Element('mujoco',model='OWN MOD4 current MATLAB reference ghost figure')
        ET.SubElement(root,'compiler',angle='radian')
        visual=ET.SubElement(root,'visual');ET.SubElement(visual,'global',offwidth='2200',offheight='1150')
        ET.SubElement(visual,'headlight',ambient='.65 .65 .65',diffuse='.6 .6 .6')
        ET.SubElement(visual,'rgba',haze='.04 .055 .075 1')
        asset=ET.SubElement(root,'asset');meshes=asset0.findall('mesh');assert len(meshes)==277
        for mesh in meshes:
            node=copy.deepcopy(mesh);node.set('file',str((base/'meshes'/node.get('file')).resolve()));asset.append(node)
        ET.SubElement(asset,'texture',name='grid',type='2d',builtin='checker',rgb1='.07 .10 .13',rgb2='.11 .15 .19',width='512',height='512')
        ET.SubElement(asset,'material',name='gridmat',texture='grid',texrepeat='10 10',reflectance='.06')
        world=ET.SubElement(root,'worldbody')
        ET.SubElement(world,'light',pos='-.5 -2 3',dir='.2 .3 -1',diffuse='.8 .85 1')
        ET.SubElement(world,'geom',name='floor',type='plane',size='4 4 .02',material='gridmat',contype='0',conaffinity='0')
        ET.SubElement(world,'geom',name='platform_270mm',type='box',pos='.75 0 .135',size='.4 .6 .135',rgba='.34 .38 .43 1',contype='0',conaffinity='0')
        ET.SubElement(world,'geom',name='platform_lip',type='box',pos='.35 0 .2705',size='.002 .6 .0015',rgba='1 .66 .18 1',contype='0',conaffinity='0')
        colors=[];count=0
        cmap=plt.get_cmap('cool')
        for j,(age,pose) in enumerate(zip(ages,poses)):
            rgb=np.asarray(cmap(j/(len(ages)-1)))[:3]
            alpha=.28 if j not in (0,4,8) else .78
            if j==8:rgb=np.array([.77,.83,.88]);alpha=.95
            colors.append(rgb.tolist())
            for name in body_names:
                body=ET.SubElement(world,'body',name=f'ghost{j}_{name}',mocap='true')
                for geom in bodies[name].findall('geom'):
                    node=copy.deepcopy(geom);node.set('name',f'ghost{j}_'+node.get('name'));node.set('density','0');node.set('contype','0');node.set('conaffinity','0');node.set('rgba',' '.join(map(str,[*rgb,alpha])));body.append(node);count+=1
        com=[];wheel_low=[]
        masses=np.array([C['bodies'][name]['mass_kg'] for name in body_names])
        for row in table:
            pose=fk(row)
            centres=np.array([pose[name][0]+pose[name][1]@np.asarray(C['bodies'][name]['com_m']) for name in body_names])
            com.append((centres*masses[:,None]).sum(0)/masses.sum())
            low=[]
            for name in ('left_wheel','right_wheel'):
                shape=next(s for s in G['shapes'] if s['body']==name);p,r=pose[name]
                points=np.asarray(shape['points'])@np.asarray(shape['linear_body_from_shape']).T+np.asarray(shape['origin_body_m'])
                points=points@r.T+p;low.append(points[points[:,2].argmin()])
            wheel_low.append(low)
        com=np.array(com);wheel_low=np.array(wheel_low)
        for a,b in zip(com[493:678:3],com[496:681:3]):
            ET.SubElement(world,'geom',type='capsule',fromto=' '.join(map(str,np.r_[a,b])),size='.0022',rgba='.1 .93 .81 .85',contype='0',conaffinity='0')
        xml=ET.tostring(root,encoding='unicode');(D/'CURRENT_TRUE_MOD4_GHOST_SCENE.xml').write_text(xml,encoding='utf-8')
        model=mujoco.MjModel.from_xml_string(xml);data=mujoco.MjData(model)
        assert model.nq==model.nv==0 and model.nmocap==31*len(ages) and count==277*len(ages)
        for j,pose in enumerate(poses):
            for name in body_names:
                mid=model.body_mocapid[mujoco.mj_name2id(model,mujoco.mjtObj.mjOBJ_BODY,f'ghost{j}_{name}')]
                data.mocap_pos[mid]=pose[name][0];mujoco.mju_mat2Quat(data.mocap_quat[mid],pose[name][1].ravel())
        mujoco.mj_forward(model,data)
        cam=mujoco.MjvCamera();mujoco.mjv_defaultCamera(cam)
        cam.lookat[:]=[.12,-.095,.42];cam.distance=2.7;cam.azimuth=86;cam.elevation=-12
        with mujoco.Renderer(model,height=1150,width=2200,max_geom=10000) as renderer:
            renderer.update_scene(data,camera=cam);rgb=renderer.render().copy()
        fig=plt.figure(figsize=(15.5,10),facecolor='#0b111a')
        ax=fig.add_axes([0,.21,1,.72]);ax.imshow(rgb);ax.axis('off')
        fig.text(.045,.96,'MOD4 · MATLAB 数学候选轨迹',fontproperties=font,color='#e9f2fb',fontsize=23,weight='bold')
        fig.text(.045,.925,'真实 CAD 模型的关键姿态残影   /   尚未通过动力学验证',fontproperties=font,color='#94a8bd',fontsize=12)
        fig.text(.78,.925,'台阶高度 270 mm',fontproperties=font,color='#f1b653',fontsize=13)
        ax2=fig.add_axes([.08,.09,.61,.16],facecolor='#0b111a')

        low=wheel_low[:, :,2].min(1)
        ax2.plot(table[493:678,0],low[493:678],color='#90a4ff',lw=2,label='双轮最低点高度')
        ax2.plot([.35,1.15],[.27,.27],color='#e5ae52',lw=2,label='27cm 台面')
        ax2.plot([.35,.35],[0,.27],color='#e5ae52',lw=2)
        for j,k in enumerate(ages):ax2.scatter(com[k,0],com[k,2],s=18,color=colors[j])
        ax2.set_xlim(-.65,.85);ax2.set_ylim(-.02,.85);ax2.tick_params(colors='#9aacc0',labelsize=9)
        for spine in ax2.spines.values():spine.set_color('#273749')
        ax2.grid(alpha=.12,color='#bacde0');ax2.set_xlabel('世界 X / m',fontproperties=font,color='#bacde0');ax2.set_ylabel('世界 Z / m',fontproperties=font,color='#bacde0')
        ax2.legend(prop=font,labelcolor='#b8cadd',frameon=False,fontsize=8,loc='upper left')
        fig.text(.73,.19,'加速 → 压腿 → 起跳\n腾空收腿 → 落地 → 恢复',fontproperties=font,color='#d4e1ee',fontsize=13,linespacing=1.8)
        fig.text(.73,.105,'分镜：4.93 – 6.77 s\n当前仍在检查质心、接触和力矩条件',fontproperties=font,color='#91a6bb',fontsize=10,linespacing=1.6)
        buf=io.BytesIO();fig.savefig(buf,format='png',dpi=170,facecolor=fig.get_facecolor());plt.close(fig)
        png=buf.getvalue();out=D/'MOD4_CURRENT_MATLAB_GHOST_TRAJECTORY.png';out.write_bytes(png)
        put(D/'TRUE_MOD4_CURRENT_REFERENCE_RENDER_ACTUAL.json',dict(passed=True,reads=reads,visual_source_XML=str(base/'live_display.xml'),visual_XML_SHA=hashlib.sha256(source_xml).hexdigest(),original_visual_meshes=277,body_names=body_names,ghost_reference_ages=ages,rendered_visual_geoms=count,mujoco_version=mujoco.__version__,physics_mj_step_calls=0,neural_forward_calls=0,neural_checkpoint_or_old_raw_reads=0,full_true_MOD4_geometry_not_substitute=True,source_is_mathematical_reference_not_PhysX_recording=True,physical_teacher_valid=False,output=dict(path=str(out),bytes=len(png),SHA=hashlib.sha256(png).hexdigest(),producer_in_memory_hash_no_binary_reread=True)))
        print(json.dumps(dict(passed=True,image=str(out),bytes=len(png),SHA=hashlib.sha256(png).hexdigest(),true_visual_meshes=277,ghosts=len(ages)),ensure_ascii=False))
